- completed — commit `d6ea9661` (22 files, +326/−25). Reason: V4 PD runs need table-priced steps because no qualified per-operator V4 model exists. Method: new `frontier/execution_time_predictor/dsv4_step_cost_input.py` binds `dsv4_step_cost.json` next to the resolved CUDA_EVENT attention input, only for the V4 profile with dummy mode off, and checks schema, qualification, model, device and topology. `deepseek_v4.predict_step_cost_execution` spreads the lane step over the layers as one attention operator; FFN, EP waves, idle forwards and CPU work cost 0. V4 PREFILL and unified DECODE use the co-location V4 layer path; table-priced clusters skip sklearn training; role configs inherit `attention_backend` and the two KV-cache dtypes. Native needs 0.81 more steps on average than the rule: late handles among the 8 (design section 6.2, open).
- finding — part b (13.61 ms) is 13.41 ms of wait from the D API arrival to the engine update before the `add`, then 0.20 ms to the `add`. Frontier's wait for the running lane step already covers the engine-loop wait; the non-engine ingress (a, about 4 ms of b, g) is about 8.6 ms.

Native runs `AsyncScheduler` with batch queue depth 2 (vLLM 0.28 `v1/engine/core.py:209,624`; placeholders `v1/core/sched/async_scheduler.py:19-49`). Depth 2 delays the READ post by one step and makes f two steps. Earlier mechanism notes cited vLLM 0.16.0 line numbers; the design cites the native version 0.28.0 (`/data/ycfeng/envs/oneshot-opd-py312`).

Reason: item 1 is the core D-H4A-CHECKPOINT (b) mechanism and is testable alone; item 2 changes an accepted `max_concurrent_batches` range and in-flight token accounting and needs its own design.
- decision (D-NO-INTERRUPT) — recommend option B: a new KV-transfer field `nixl_read_completion` (default `transfer_end`, today's path). Reason: GLM, co-location, PDD and PD-AF stay identical as recorded; a later GLM diagnostic decides whether the new path replaces the old one. - completed — commit `5f907f66`. Reason: model the native NIXL READ completion at DECODE engine steps. Expectation: the default `transfer_end` is unchanged; `engine_step` adds about 2·S_F − T to TTFT. Method: new field `AnalyticalKVCacheTransferConfig.nixl_read_completion`; in `engine_step` the READ is registered on the DECODE lane at P end, the lane's next step start posts it, a later step start at or after its wire end finds it, and that step's output reports it; an idle lane without DP wakes at the wire end. Unit tests `tests/unit/test_nixl_read_completion.py`.
- finding — livelock. The first engine_step runs at `5f907f66` stopped advancing (native06 probe: simulated time 0.287354 s for 100 s of wall time, events 77,439 → 469,672; stopped, exit 143). Cause: a posted READ keeps the MoE DP wave running, and the DSV4 step-cost path prices a dummy forward at zero, so an all-dummy group ran its passes at one simulated time.
- decision (D-NO-INTERRUPT, lead's recommendation) — option (a): when a dummy forward ends at its issue time and no lane of the group has a request to schedule, the next pass starts at the earliest posted wire end. Rejected (b) a nonzero dummy-forward cost, because no measured cost exists and it would change the default DSV4 diagnostics; rejected (c) not counting posted READs, because vLLM counts WAITING_FOR_REMOTE_KVS requests as unfinished. Commit `96d9c1ef`. Total 8 `frontier/` files, +202/−9, inside the approved bound; `vllm_v1_engine_replica_scheduler.py` has 1,894 lines.

- completed — item 2 design, [h4b_d_side_model/design.md](h4b_d_side_model/design.md) section 10, from code reading of vLLM 0.28.0 (`/data/ycfeng/envs/oneshot-opd-py312`) and Frontier at `e33c24a5`; no `frontier/` edit. Findings it rests on: vLLM sets depth 2 for async scheduling with PP ≤ 1 (`config/vllm.py:550-560`); placeholders give an in-flight decoding request one new token per step (`async_scheduler.py:19-49`, `scheduler.py:558-561`); deferred block free affects only requests freed with a step in flight (`scheduler.py:2428-2440`); Frontier's DP and non-DP engine loops already fill to capacity and block on the oldest batch; the base DECODE loop's `_continuation_request_ids` keeps a request out of a second batch of the same call.
- decision (D-NO-INTERRUPT, lead's recommendation) — gate item 2 with a DECODE role override of the existing `max_concurrent_batches` through the unchanged `_OVERRIDE_FIELDS` registry; on unified DECODE an explicit 2 means output placeholders. Reason: no second way to set depth 2, PREFILL and MONOLITHIC unchanged, default path identical by construction. Rejected: a new `async_scheduling` flag (a second way), and placeholder semantics on MONOLITHIC capacity 2 (changes accepted MONOLITHIC results and needs native MONOLITHIC evidence; recorded as a separate fidelity item).
- decision (D-NO-INTERRUPT) — one READ rule at every depth: a schedule pass claims the READs registered before it, the claiming batch's start posts them, every start finds READs whose wire end has passed and keeps them under that step, and that step's output pop or dummy-forward end reports them. Reason: at depth 2 item 1's lane-wide lists post and report one step early. At depth 1 the rule differs from item 1 only for a READ registered at the same simulated time after a pass; verification step 2 measures it.
- decision (D-NO-INTERRUPT) — no fail-fast for a preempted request in two in-flight batches; an earlier draft of the design had one. Reason: schedule passes, the only preemption sites, run only with a free queue slot, so at capacity 2 a victim is in at most one in-flight batch, which the existing `step_in_flight` path handles; a guard for an unreachable state is not added. The deferred block free is a stated simplification.
- decision (D-NO-INTERRUPT) — move the NIXL READ table to a new mixin `NixlReadCompletion` (`vllm_v1_nixl_reads.py`). Reason: keep `vllm_v1_engine_replica_scheduler.py` (1,894 lines) below 2,000 after item 2.
- decision (D-NO-INTERRUPT) — PREFILL depth 2 (P engines also run `--async-scheduling`) is a candidate item 3, not part of item 2. Reason: separate role and evidence; it may explain part of the P2-32 P-side +45.92 ms.
- resolved — YC approved item 2 ("批准，继续"; D-H4B-ITEM2, requirements item 22): 8 files, about 140-190 lines plus tests, the DECODE role field. Item 3 and the ingress field are not approved.
- completed — commit `82390e10` (parent `9b8aaf17`), 11 `frontier/` files (1 new) +236/−65 and 6 test files. Reason: native DECODE engines run vLLM's AsyncScheduler with batch queue depth 2, and Frontier's capacity-1 DECODE lane made the handoff about 2·S_F short. Expectation (design section 10.8): TTFT native06 404.03, P2-32 398.38, P2-64 697.09 ms; TPOT nearly unchanged; default path identical. Method: role field `decode_replica_scheduler_config_max_concurrent_batches` (`omit_if_none`) through the unchanged `_OVERRIDE_FIELDS` registry; an explicit value on unified DECODE uses output placeholders; the READ table moves to the mixin `NixlReadCompletion` with one claim-post-find-report rule at every depth. `vllm_v1_engine_replica_scheduler.py` has 1,914 lines.
- finding and fix — the second in-flight batch was dropped as stale (28 warnings in an early probe; that log was not kept). Cause: `Batch` records `current_decode_token_index` at formation, and the first batch's output advances it before the second ends. Decision (D-NO-INTERRUPT, lead's recommendation): `Batch` takes `num_output_placeholders` (vLLM's name) and the expected index adds it; only placeholder lanes pass nonzero counts. Reason: it is the native semantics and leaves every other lane's check unchanged. Deviation: design section 10.6 said `Batch` is unchanged.
- decision (D-NO-INTERRUPT) — the DECODE validation requires FCFS. Reason: under FCFS the preemption victim is the running tail, in at most one in-flight batch, which the existing path handles; a priority victim may already be in a batch of the same pass. Every DSV4 case uses FCFS; no new guard was added.
- decision (D-NO-INTERRUPT) — the design's DECODE prefix-caching check was dropped. Reason: unified DECODE has no prefix-caching manager, so the check cannot fail.
- decision (D-NO-INTERRUPT) — the grouped admission formula `max_in_flight_tokens = max_concurrent_batches × max_tokens_in_batch` applies on every lane, not only placeholder lanes. Reason: grouped allocation is PP1-only, so every other lane has capacity 1 and the value is unchanged; the 77-case fidelity matrix is identical.
- finding — the shorthand `--decode_replica_scheduler_config_max_concurrent_batches` of design section 10.9 is rejected by the H4a-4 driver; the flat name is `cluster_config_decode_replica_scheduler_config_max_concurrent_batches`.
- deviation — size: 11 files +236/−65 against the approved 8 files and about 140-190 lines. The three extra files are `batch.py` and `base_replica_scheduler.py` (the stale-batch fix) and the help text in `kv_cache_transfer_config.py`; the READ table move counts on both sides. Recorded here and reported to YC under D-NO-INTERRUPT.
